// Codigo para las flechas a los costados
$('#_samewidth_images').owlCarousel({
      margin:10,
      autoWidth:false,
      nav:true,
      
  })

function closeBox(){
  let tabcontent, tablinks;

  tabcontent = document.getElementsByClassName("tabcontent");
  for (i = 0; i < tabcontent.length; i++) {
    tabcontent[i].style.display = "none";
    }

  tablinks = document.getElementsByClassName("tablinks");
  for (i = 0; i < tablinks.length; i++) {
    tablinks[i].className = tablinks[i].className.replace(" active", "");
  }

    //saca la flecha de abajo
   flecha = document.getElementsByClassName("flechad");
    for (i = 0; i < flecha.length; i++) {
      flecha[i].className = flecha[i].className.replace("flechad", "");
    }
}

// Filtro

$( '.owl-filter-bar' ).on( 'click', '.item', function() {

    var $item = $(this);
    var filter = $item.data( 'owl-filter' )
closeBox()
    owl.owlcarousel2_filter( filter );

} )


</script>

<!-- scripts para los textos del carrusel-->
<script>
    function openCity(evt, cityName, arrow) {

  var i, tabcontent, tablinks, flecha, blanco, blancoder, blancoizq;



  if(evt.target.className === "tablinks"){

      tabcontent = document.getElementsByClassName("tabcontent");
      for (i = 0; i < tabcontent.length; i++) {
        tabcontent[i].style.display = "none";
      }

      tablinks = document.getElementsByClassName("tablinks");
      for (i = 0; i < tablinks.length; i++) {
        tablinks[i].className = tablinks[i].className.replace(" active", "");
      }

      //saca la flecha de abajo
       flecha = document.getElementsByClassName("flechad");
        for (i = 0; i < flecha.length; i++) {
          flecha[i].className = flecha[i].className.replace("flechad", "");
        }

      document.getElementById(arrow).className=" flechad";      
      document.getElementById(cityName).style.display = "block";
      evt.currentTarget.className += " active";

  }
  else{
      tabcontent = document.getElementsByClassName("tabcontent");
      for (i = 0; i < tabcontent.length; i++) {
        tabcontent[i].style.display = "none";
      }

      tablinks = document.getElementsByClassName("tablinks");
      for (i = 0; i < tablinks.length; i++) {
        tablinks[i].className = tablinks[i].className.replace(" active", "");
      }

      document.getElementById(arrow).className="";

  }


  
  blanco = document.getElementsByClassName("fondoblanco")
  blancoder = document.getElementsByClassName("fondoblancoesquinader")
  blancoizq = document.getElementsByClassName("fondoblancoesquinaizq")
//izquierda
if(arrow === 'arrow1' || arrow === 'arrow6' || arrow === 'arrow11' || arrow === 'arrow16' || arrow === 'arrow21' || arrow === 'arrow26' || arrow === 'arrow31' || arrow === 'arrow36' || arrow === 'arrow41' || arrow === 'arrow46' || arrow === 'arrow51' || arrow === 'arrow56' || arrow === 'arrow61' || arrow === 'arrow66' || arrow === 'arrow71' || arrow === 'arrow76' || arrow === 'arrow81' || arrow === 'arrow86' || arrow === 'arrow91' || arrow === 'arrow96' || arrow === 'arrow101' || arrow === 'arrow106' || arrow === 'arrow111' || arrow === 'arrow116' || arrow === 'arrow121' || arrow === 'arrow126' || arrow === 'arrow131' || arrow === 'arrow1000'  || arrow === 'arrow6000' || arrow === 'arrow9000' || arrow === 'arrow14000' || arrow === 'arrow18000' || arrow === 'arrow23000' || arrow === 'arrow28000' || arrow === 'arrow33000' || arrow === 'arrow34000' || arrow === 'arrow36000' || arrow === 'arrow41000' || arrow === 'arrow43000' || arrow === 'arrow48000' || arrow === 'arrow53000' || arrow === 'arrow58000' || arrow === 'arrow60000' || arrow === 'arrow62000' || arrow === 'arrow63000' || arrow === 'arrow70000' || arrow === 'arrow75000' || arrow === 'arrow78000' || arrow === 'arrow83000' || arrow === 'arrow65000' || arrow === 'arrow88000' || arrow === 'arrow91000' || arrow === 'arrow92000' || arrow === 'arrow97000' || arrow === 'arrow101000' || arrow === 'arrow102000' || arrow === 'arrow107000' || arrow === 'arrow112000' || arrow === 'arrow117000' || arrow === 'arrow122000' || arrow === 'arrow127000' || arrow === 'arrow131000' || arrow === 'arrow135000'){


for (i = 0; i < blanco.length; i++) {

 blanco[i].className = blanco[i].className.replace("fondoblanco", "fondoblancoesquinaizq");

 }
for (i = 0; i < blancoder.length; i++) {

 blancoder[i].className = blancoder[i].className.replace("fondoblancoesquinader", "fondoblancoesquinaizq");

 }


}

//derecha
else if(arrow === 'arrow5' || arrow === 'arrow10' || arrow === 'arrow15' || arrow === 'arrow20' || arrow === 'arrow25' || arrow === 'arrow30' || arrow === 'arrow35' || arrow === 'arrow40' || arrow === 'arrow45' || arrow === 'arrow50' || arrow === 'arrow55' || arrow === 'arrow60' || arrow === 'arrow65' || arrow === 'arrow70' || arrow === 'arrow75' || arrow === 'arrow80' || arrow === 'arrow85' || arrow === 'arrow90' || arrow === 'arrow95' || arrow === 'arrow100' || arrow === 'arrow105' || arrow === 'arrow110' || arrow === 'arrow115' || arrow === 'arrow120' || arrow === 'arrow125' || arrow === 'arrow130' || arrow === 'arrow135' || arrow === 'arrow5000' || arrow === 'arrow13000' || arrow === 'arrow22000' || arrow === 'arrow27000' || arrow === 'arrow32000' || arrow === 'arrow47000' || arrow === 'arrow52000' || arrow === 'arrow57000' || arrow === 'arrow69000' || arrow === 'arrow74000' || arrow === 'arrow82000' || arrow === 'arrow87000' || arrow === 'arrow96000' || arrow === 'arrow106000' || arrow === 'arrow110000' || arrow === 'arrow116000' || arrow === 'arrow121000' || arrow === 'arrow126000' || arrow === 'arrow40000'){ 


for (i = 0; i < blanco.length; i++) {

 blanco[i].className = blanco[i].className.replace("fondoblanco", "fondoblancoesquinader");

 }

 for (i = 0; i < blancoizq.length; i++) {

 blancoizq[i].className = blancoizq[i].className.replace("fondoblancoesquinaizq", "fondoblancoesquinader");

 }


}





else {

  blancoizq = document.getElementsByClassName("fondoblancoesquinaizq")
  blancoder = document.getElementsByClassName("fondoblancoesquinader")

for (i = 0; i < blancoizq.length; i++) {
  
blancoizq[i].className = blancoizq[i].className.replace("fondoblancoesquinaizq", "fondoblanco");
}
for (i = 0; i < blancoder.length; i++) {
  
blancoder[i].className = blancoder[i].className.replace("fondoblancoesquinader", "fondoblanco");
}

 }

}

const navLetras = document.querySelector('nav.owl-filter-bar');
const header = document.getElementById('main-navigation');

const headerAltura = header.offsetHeight;
const navLetrasInicio = navLetras.getBoundingClientRect().top;

const letrasSeleccionadasAnteriormente = [ ];
navLetras.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    if(letrasSeleccionadasAnteriormente.length) letrasSeleccionadasAnteriormente.forEach(letra => letra.removeAttribute('style'));
    e.target.style = "outline: 2px solid var(--color_principal); outline-offset: 5px;";
    letrasSeleccionadasAnteriormente.push(e.target);
    setTimeout(() => {
        if (window.location.href[window.location.href.length - 1] === '#') containerCarrusel.scrollIntoView(false); //  scrollTo(0,(navLetrasInicio - headerAltura))
    }, 1000)

});

</script>

        $('.tb-megamenu-button').on('click', function() {

<script>
	const linkMenu = document.querySelector("li[data-id='2966']>a");
linkMenu.textContent = "";
	const titulo1 = document.createTextNode("Autoridades ");
	const br = document.createElement("br");
	const titulo2 = document.createTextNode(" Áreas de Gobierno");
	linkMenu.appendChild(titulo1);
	linkMenu.appendChild(br);
	linkMenu.appendChild(titulo2);

	let btnMapaEstado = document.querySelector("li[data-id='4633']>a");
	btnMapaEstado.setAttribute("target", "_blank");
	document.querySelector("button[data-target='.nav-collapse']").setAttribute("aria-label", "Abrir menú");
	//  Script que permite desplegar en mobile los submenues del header (Institucional y Casa de Gobierno)

	if (window.matchMedia("(max-width: 840px)").matches) {

		let subMenuInstitucional = document.querySelector("div#block-tb-megamenu-menu-menu-principal-prueba>.content>.tb-megamenu>.nav-collapse .tb-megamenu-submenu.dropdown-menu.mega-dropdown-menu.nav-child");

		// Descomentar cuando el link de Casa de Gobierno esté visible		
		//let subMenuCasaGobierno = document.querySelector("div#tb-megamenu-column-2>.tb-megamenu-column-inner> .tb-megamenu-subnav .tb-megamenu-submenu.nav-child")

		let btnCruz = document.querySelector(".tb-megamenu-button");
		let btnInstitucional = document.querySelector("li[data-id='4632']>a");

		// Descomentar cuando el link de Casa de Gobierno esté visible. Primero verificar data-id.		
		//let btnCasaGobierno = document.querySelector("li[data-id='4570']>a");
		btnInstitucional.setAttribute('href', 'javascript:void(0)');

		// Descomentar cuando el link de Casa de Gobierno esté visible
		//btnCasaGobierno.setAttribute('href', 'javascript:void(0)');

		btnInstitucional.addEventListener('click', () => {
			subMenuInstitucional.classList.toggle("open_submenu");
		});

		// Descomentar cuando el link de Casa de Gobierno esté visible
		/*btnCasaGobierno.addEventListener('click', () => {
			subMenuCasaGobierno.classList.toggle("open-submenu")
		})*/

		btnCruz.addEventListener('click', () => {
			if (subMenuInstitucional.classList.contains("open_submenu"))
				subMenuInstitucional.classList.remove("open_submenu");

			// Descomentar cuando el link de Casa de Gobierno esté visible
			/*if(subMenuCasaGobierno.classList.contains("open-submenu"))
				subMenuCasaGobierno.classList.remove("open-submenu");*/
		})
	}
</script>

	if (w < 480) {
